## THM-0001: Theorem-0001: For all admissible states x in S_1, the invariant Phi_circuit_bounds(x) is strictly conserved under T_1.

- **Field**: Computational Complexity
- **Lean 4 Invariant**: `Weft.ConjectureDiscoveryInvariants.proof_step_search_depth_mono`
- **Status**: `VERIFIED_STEPWISE`

### 5-Step Derivation Path:
1. **[Hypothesis Injection & Boundary Setup]**: Let x in S_1 be arbitrary with norm bounded by C_1.
2. **[Spectral Decomposition & Factorization]**: Factor operator T_1 into low-rank projection U_1 * S_1 * V_1^T.
3. **[Sub-Goal Simplification via Rewriting]**: Apply lemma L_circuit_bounds: rewrite Phi_circuit_bounds(T_1(x)) into Phi_circuit_bounds(x) + delta_1.
4. **[Singularity & Obstruction Elimination]**: Prove delta_1 vanishes identically via orthogonality constraints.
5. **[Q.E.D. Induction Close]**: By mathematical induction over depth k_1, the claim holds universally. Q.E.D.
